  I have been researching the Crop Circle mystery now for twenty years and I                           have learned a lot
  about bent plants and some unbelievable things that are                     going on around them.


  The field of crop circle research has become very confused over the past                      several years with
   people making claims that do not stand up tp rigorous                   scrutiny.   In this·article I will review
   areas of research from the early        days of the phenomenon, discussing the                   'sWirl signature ' ,
  which I believe      to be very important in distinguishing genuine crop formations. Regrettably
   I also need to mention the current level of myth-making and deception coming                                  from within the
   research community. I will also touch upon the undeniable                    evidence that through interaction
  with the crop circle phenomenon, we have              made contact with non-human mind.


   My first book,    'Circular EVidence' , co-authored with Pat Delgado, was                     published in 1989 and

  is acknowledged as the        "Crop Circle Bible."        In that      book we placed the building blocks into
   position for this new subject of       study called crop circles. This new subject regularly displayed

new    patterns and new anomalies that were given new names in the English                  language. We

coined the terms      'Crop Circle ' ,     'Pictogram'    and      'Agrtglyph' , which are now household
words. We recorded the primary           physical features and properties of the circles , such as the bent
stems of     involved plants and changes in seed germination rates. We reported the first                small
globe shaped U.F.O' s seen and filmed around the circles; the first              magnetic anomalies; and the
first scientific plant analysis at HSC       Laboratories. We traced the phenomenon around the world
and mapped its      geographical occurrence. These early days were groundbreaking times and our
initial results have become the foundation upon which the current phenomenon                    rests.


Although we know from folklore and anecdotal evidence that the Crop Circle                 history is a long
one, the earliest photographed reports in my possession             start in 1966.    These appeared in      ·

Australia and were followed by more in          1975. Photographs from circles in Switzerland date back
to 1975 and 1976.        The first photographs of crop circles in England were taken in Hampshire in
1978. Since then, more and more photographic evidence demonstrates                    worldwide activity with a
focus in southern England.


I personally saw my first set of circles in southern England during the              summer of 1983. At that
time, unbeknownst to me, two other men were              beginning to investigate. In 1980, fourteen years
after the first report    arrived from Australia, only one person was intrigued enough to begin
asking     questions about them. That person was meteorologist Dr. Terence Meaden. It                    was a
year later that electro-mechanical designs engineer Pat Delgado saw               his first circles in
Hampshire and began his inquiries. I became the third               person who was curious enough to
begin a disciplined study, and together we         became the first team of researchers. I was in a
privileged government job       that gave me access to the police_ and other agencies, which was very
helpful    in obtaining site reports. Light-aircraft pilot Busty Taylor joined our          efforts in the
summer of 1985 after seeing a crop formation while flying his             aircraft. The team was now
complete and the real work was underway. Our               research consisted of aerial photography , site
inspection and     straightforward detective work. (Later , in 1999, when hoaxing had a become a
major obstacle to the research, I hired professional detective agencies to            better understand the
impact of man-made circles.)




Although the world has become enamored of the giant complex formations                   featured in books and

the media , the genuine phenomenon revealed itself            through simpler fqrmations. In the 1980 ' s
we were recording an evolution       of multiple simple circles arranged together in patterns. Starting
with   single circles we next found circles with a neighbor , forming twin sets;           then three circles
in a straight line; then five circles arranged in a       Celtic cross. During these early days when the
phenomenon was less         contaminated by man-made formations we saw only simple geometries.
Even      during the late 1980' s the formations evolved in complexity no further than              circles with
single , double and triple concentric rings. Another development            of the late 80' s was the

appearance of numerous very small circles we             called   'grapeshot '   that were scattered across
entire regions. They varied     in size from just a few inches to a few feet, much smaller than the
typical    circles that ranged from 60 feet to around 100 feet across.


May 1990 changed everything when two circles were discovered connected by a                   straight pathway
with parallel boxes either side of the pathway. This            discovery was the birth of Pictograms , and
after this first Pictogram was     born, nothing was the same again. The media and public attention
forever    changed the forum in which we worked, bringing a degree qf human involvement                     and
copy-cat circle-making the level of which we are still working to           uncover. Because of this, I like
to refer to the early days of the research       as the uncontaminated phase, when information
gathering was less distorted      by human involvement: less people making them , less people
trampling the     fields and destroying the evidence.

Through the years I have looked closely at the clues found in the fields,                  loo}tlng for footprints,
plant and soil compression. damage and other                    evidence of human presence. I have investigated
along an entire spectrum of           research initiatives, looking for insights into the phenomenon. I
have sent      plants and soil for analysis; worked with scientists from Japan measuring                   brain
wave actiVity of people inside the circles; taken infrared                photographs; conducted electrostatic
and electromagnetic site suiVeys; and              taken many other, less traditional, research initiatives. It
is worth     mentioning that I am an electrical engineer by profession. and qualified to                 design and
conduct research of this kind. Our original team has been able                     to demonstrate that there is a
genuine phenomenon with identifiable                features. In addition to that we have recorded significant
anomalous        events. To this day, what intrigues me the most is not that anomalies abound                    in
the circles, but that they exist in the genuine and man-made ci rcles                   both.




There are some distinct differences in the features of the formations we                   studied in the less
contaminated phase of the research than is found in the                    patterns we see today. These features
are key to understanding the degree of              human involvement in present day crop circle

formations. One primary            feature is found in the          'sWirl signature ' . The signature is a clear

belief that the genuine phenomenon does still exist                                      but is sadly overshadowed by the degree and
complexity of man-made designs.

There have been many attempts to replicate the complex svvirl patterns found                                in genuine crop
circles but ALL have failed. The media has sponsored               circle-making competitions, has put forth
challenges, has created circles to     dupe researchers and trap them in error, yet no one has
reproduced the swirl      floor lay of the original simple circles.        Circle-makers have been able to
reproduce complex designs at night while being filmed; they have created                             giant patten1s in only
four hotirs, again being filmed; they have reproduced              complex geometries; but no one has

reproduced the features found in the       original simple circles. Let' s take a look at a few examples

of the     'signature'    I am referring to.
Compare the swirl signatures of Fig 7-11 and
note the marked difference with        the man­
made circles made by Doug Bowers and Dave
Chorley in the late 1980s      (Fig 12 and Fig15).
Also look at the design made by Matthew

Williams in 2000       (Fig 13). The   'swirl
signature'     of the genuine circles reveals a
rotation     from the center of the circle to
the outside edge of less than two
rotations, usually approximately one
rotation.     The man-made circles leave
numerous swathes, equally spaced and
rotating many times across the profile          of the
circle, related to the width of the tool used to         Fig 1 5
                                                         One o f the man-made counterfeits at Cheesefoot Head, Hampshire, 1986.
compress the plants.       These swathes are not         This one did not have the swirl signature of the real one nearby(See fig 9).


present in the genuine phenomenon (Fig' s 7,
8, 9, 10    and 11).


                                                     -10-
It has become clear to me that there is a distinct difference between the           man-made formations
and the real formations and yet It is also clear to me          that both are part of the phenomenon and
together create a genuine mystery.        Myself and others have reported many times that the
phenomenon interacts          with human intention, often responding to visualized patterns or foiling
well-developed theories. On many occasions real crop circles have occurred              in the same field and
at the same time as man-made formations, and often               have a relationship to each other.
Cheesefoot Head, Hampshire in 1986 is          one such example as is the picture on the cover of

 'Circular Evidence'     at     Longstock, 1988.    Interestingly, the real formations in both of these
examples display the swirl signature, while the man-made formations do not.               Many human
circle-makers feel compelled to make designs, discovering            afterwards a link to a larger event. In

my new book,      'Signs of Contact" , I    delve into these kinds of interactions taking place between
the observers     and the observed.     I believe that a non-human intelligence is at work and
that whether the observer is a researcher or a person constructing circles.            physical
manifestations are responding to their minds and intentions. This             possibility has enormous
implications for mankind and perhaps, our future.


My book,      'Crop Circles ? Signs of Contact' , published by Career Press is         due out :March 2003.
In the book I have the opportunity to thoroughly develop            these ideas and present the full

evidence.    In addition, every crop pattern    ever discovered and recorded in the        'Andrews

Catalogue '    will be published     in the book.   This will be the first time all the patterns that have
been   discovered worldwide will be made available in one place.


After two decades of researching this extraordinary mystery I have come to             two conclusions.
First, a substanti� number of circles around the world are             genuine, but many more complex
designs are man-made. Second, there is         interaction occurring through the phenomenon between
a non-human       intelligence and us. This interaction creates enormous implications for
humankind. To fully realize the degree and importance of the interactive             nature of this
phenomenon we need to open better dialogue between                researchers and circle-makers. When
people on two different sides of a      debate are not talking to each other, important conclusions
                                                                                                                  �J
can be missed.     The people who are making circles and the people who are researching them

are not communicating and therefore the extent of interaction with this             non-human mind
cannot be shared, or in some cases even recognized, by the             individuals involved. The fact that
these people on different sides of the      fence each have experienced vital pieces of the puzzle is
ironic and     symbolic too.


It is with sincere regret I have to mention the level of distortion of       information coming from
within the crop circle research community. In recent            years the level of myth making surrounding
the subject has grown      exponentially and on occasions I have reason to wonder if some of the
hype   constitutes deliberate deception for reasons of profit.         These are strong   suggestions I

know, but the importance of what this subject could mean              demands honesty. I don' t want to
fully engage this aspect of the subject      in this article, but I will show one important example of
how the public is     deceived. If we have something of importance going on, then we need to be
dealing in truth not exaggerated claims.


During the summer of 2002 most researches, including myself, received an                e-mail from a well
known human circle-maker.          This man and his team are        responsible for making some of the
more elaborate crop formations in the UK.           The email announced that the circle-makers would be
making a complex face         design in the crops during the weekend of August 31st.        Other sources
indicated this would be done in Wiltshire.      This engendered much disbelief         due to the enormous
publicity and public interest generated by an earlier           formation of an alien face with a disc that
had appeared on the 15th of         August in Hampshire. A whole range of theories with photographs

and reports     were posted on crop circle web sites within hours of discovery of this         'ET'    face. It
\vas the focus of magazine articles and newspaper stories. There            was a strong sentiment within
the research community that it could not be          made by people.      If the human circle-makers were



                                                     -   1 1-
able to construct a face,    did it mean the alien face could be human made too? The majority of
interested people will never be able to ask that question or answer it for           themselves because
none of the crop circle web sites or crop circle     magazines will post any information about the
human-tnade face of August        31st. The tnajority of people will not see the complexity of design
nor will   they know that not only one such face appeared in the fields that weekend,            but four
identical faces appeared in multiple locations (Fig 5 ). So most        people will not be able to ask the
questions and fmd the answers that may          lead to some of the most important information of our
times. That is clear     deception and censorship and the reason why we must be committed to the
truth. wherever it may take us.


I. have stated publicly in my lectures as far back as 1988. that I felt we          would inch slowly
towards the truth and meaning of all this, and that as we          did so, negative forces would be
moving at the same rate, at the same time.        I saw the cumulating point as occurring
simultaneously with the end of our       current period of evolution, represented as the point of an
arrow. \Vhen both      forces reached that place, I anticipated chaos. And yet. that is the moment
of true creativity, when important discoveries can be made and anything or              everything is
possible, but only through honest intent.


I imagined in that creative moment, negative forces would also be fully             engaged with maximum
disruption to society. I look around at these times and        think I was not so far off. I suspect the
changes that we need to assure the        survival of all earth-based species can only come about this
late in the   game by radical changes in the old systems ? as the Native Americans say:

 'A start over' .


Since the 11th September 2001 \vhen one mind-set attacked another in New York                 and
\Vashington, enormous problems have naturally surfaced. They brought                  Muslims and
Christians into perceived direct opposition: Islam and Israel         reached new levels of antagonism:
the Roman Catholic Church began to          disintegrate under sex scandals; and international big
business scandals       rocked confidence in the money markets.         All this and more left politicians
suddenly without the old ways of doing business? HUGE change in anyone' s                  terms and that is
only a part of it.


So, coincidentally or not. the world has indeed entered a period of          potential chaos just as the
crop circle research has reached a key point of         discovezy. All the structures that anchored
mankind, good or bad are being        broke away. The whole pack of cards has been thrown into the

air and it' s    this situation that ironically offers the greatest hope. The positive thing        about
these dreadfully worzying and unstable times is that the big changes            the world and the planet
needs ARE now possible because we have OUR chance               of setting new anchors in position. The.
old ones were destined for certain      catastrophe. Yes, it is true that the privileged few who have
been in    positions of power and who have profited by the old anchors are scrambling               to
maintain authority. Evezything is in place. we have reached the point of             the arrow. I am anxious·
to disclose through my new book the full extent of         what I believe to have been the crop circle
experiment that has been taking        place for many years.


 As I write this I am aware of the difficulty some will have in       comprehending or reconciling the
scale of what is going on in the wider      world with that of the crop circles. I ask only that you are
prepared to      look at the evidence and then to consider the enormous benefits to us of           using to
good effect what the crop circle experiment has iinplied. To accept           and participate with the
findings will be to contemplate a whole different        series of possibilities.


The swirl signature I have discussed here is but a small part of the           evidence of a real
phenomenon. Proof we can directly interact with another            intelligence shows that we have
enormous opportunities ahead of us for rapid         and positive change and NOW is the time to
discuss how we can take advantage         of it. These findings \vill give us hope and strength. There is
no time or      place for yet another new religion, sL.'Tiply time to embrace the faith in     ourselves.


                                                    -   1 2-
Fig 5
One of the     ' Faces' researchers knew was going to be made, b u t did not make the public d omain. Wiltshir,
1st September 2002.

In recent times I have become known for speaking %s, well let me do that             just one more time. I

am 100% certain that through the crop circles we have              'Signs of Contact'     .   We badly need
some good news, and I believe we have           found some at the 11th hour.


If lessons are learned and we can appreciate what we have going for us,            there is no question in
my mind that the study of crop circles will center           upon human consciousness and the enormous

potential for change it will bring      us. As we should know by now, to get that far it' s all down to
us.
